# R4-1 sidecar allowlist cases (not a full compatibility suite).
# Out of scope: FT.*, modules, DUMP/RESTORE, ACL, cluster/gossip, FUNCTIONS,
# unordered replies (SMEMBERS/HGETALL; multi SRANDMEMBER/ZRANDMEMBER/HRANDFIELD), SCAN cursors, time-flake TTL seconds,
# consumer-group/XREAD BLOCK flake, GEODIST/GEOPOS float nested --raw, auto XADD * ids.
#
# Format:
#   @CMD ARG...                 setup on both sides; output ignored
#   @skip reason                documented gap; not executed (honesty; do not fake pass)
#   @todo reason                deferred assert; not executed
#   LABEL|WANT|CMD ARG...       assert redis==godis==WANT (--raw)
#   LABEL|>=N|CMD ARG...        assert both sides are integers >= N
#   LABEL|<=N|CMD ARG...        assert both sides are integers <= N
# WANT tokens: $-1 = null bulk field (empty under --raw); $empty = wholly empty reply
# Placeholder {{ID}} -> process id (unique keys per run).
#
# WANT may embed \n for multi-line --raw (e.g. ZPOPMIN member\nscore).
# @allowlist PING,ECHO,SET,SETNX,GET,GETDEL,GETSET,GETEX,STRLEN,APPEND,DEL,EXISTS,INCR,DECR,INCRBY,DECRBY,INCRBYFLOAT,TYPE,MSET,MGET,MSETNX,SETRANGE,GETRANGE,SUBSTR,SETEX,PSETEX,UNLINK,HSET,HMSET,HGET,HMGET,HSETNX,HINCRBY,HINCRBYFLOAT,HSTRLEN,HLEN,HEXISTS,HDEL,HEXPIRE,HTTL,HPEXPIRE,HPTTL,HKEYS,HVALS,HRANDFIELD,HGETDEL,HGETEX,LPUSH,LPUSHX,RPUSH,RPUSHX,LLEN,LINDEX,LRANGE,LPOP,RPOP,RPOPLPUSH,LSET,LINSERT,LREM,LTRIM,LPOS,LMOVE,LMPOP,BLMOVE,SADD,SCARD,SISMEMBER,SMISMEMBER,SMOVE,SREM,SPOP,SRANDMEMBER,SUNION,SUNIONSTORE,SINTERSTORE,SDIFFSTORE,SDIFF,SINTER,SINTERCARD,ZADD,ZINCRBY,ZSCORE,ZMSCORE,ZRANK,ZREVRANK,ZRANGE,ZREVRANGE,ZRANGEBYSCORE,ZREVRANGEBYSCORE,ZRANGEBYLEX,ZRANGESTORE,ZCARD,ZREM,ZCOUNT,ZREMRANGEBYRANK,ZREMRANGEBYSCORE,ZREMRANGEBYLEX,ZUNION,ZUNIONSTORE,ZINTER,ZINTERSTORE,ZINTERCARD,ZDIFFSTORE,ZDIFF,ZPOPMIN,ZPOPMAX,ZMPOP,ZLEXCOUNT,ZRANDMEMBER,BZPOPMIN,TTL,PTTL,PEXPIRE,PEXPIREAT,EXPIRE,EXPIREAT,EXPIRETIME,PEXPIRETIME,PERSIST,TOUCH,RENAME,MOVE,XADD,XLEN,XDEL,XTRIM,XREVRANGE,XRANGE,GEOADD,GEOHASH,GEORADIUS,GEOSEARCH,SETBIT,GETBIT,BITCOUNT,BITPOS,BITOP,BITFIELD,PFADD,PFCOUNT,PFMERGE,COPY,RENAMENX,LCS,OBJECT,DBSIZE,SORT,MEMORY

# --- String / ping ---
PING|PONG|PING
ECHO|r41-ok|ECHO r41-ok
@DEL sidecar:allowlist:{{ID}}
